## Admin Page Implementation Analysis

Based on my thorough exploration of the Safecast New Map codebase, here's the complete breakdown of the admin page structure and implementation:

### 1. **Admin Pages Overview**

The project has **three main admin pages** currently implemented:

- **User Administration**: `/admin/users` - Manage users (CRUD operations)
- **Uploads Administration**: `/admin/uploads` - View and manage file uploads
- **Tracks Administration**: `/admin/tracks` - View and manage GPS tracks

### 2. **HTML Templates**

#### Main Admin User Page
- **Location**: `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-users.html`
- **Size**: ~847 lines
- **Structure**:
  - Uses embedded CSS with CSS custom properties (variables) for theming
  - Supports light/dark mode via media queries and data-theme attributes
  - Responsive design with flexbox layout
  - Self-contained HTML with embedded JavaScript

**Key Sections**:
- Navigation bar with action buttons (Add User, Delete Selected, Back to Map)
- Summary section with pagination controls and search
- Data table with sortable columns and filter inputs
- Pagination controls (top and bottom)
- Modal dialog for Add/Edit user operations

#### Admin Uploads & Tracks Pages
- **Generated dynamically** in Go handlers (not separate HTML files)
- Locations: Built inline in `main.go` at lines 5039+ and 6349+
- Generated with embedded HTML strings in Go code

### 3. **Go Handler Structure**

#### Route Registration (`pkg/httpapi/register.go`)
```
/api/admin/users         → AdminListUsersHandler (GET)
/api/admin/users/create  → AdminCreateUserHandler (POST)
/api/admin/users/{id}    → AdminUpdateUserHandler (PUT/PATCH)
/api/admin/users/{id}    → AdminDeleteUserHandler (DELETE)
/api/admin/users/{id}/reset-password    → AdminResetUserPasswordHandler (POST)
/api/admin/users/{id}/regenerate-api-key → AdminRegenerateAPIKeyHandler (POST)
```

#### Page Serving Routes (`cmd/unified-server/main.go`)
```
/admin/users  → Serves HTML template from embedded content
/admin/uploads → Calls adminUploadsHandler (generates HTML with embedded JS)
```

#### Handler Functions Location
- **Admin User Handlers**: `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pkg/auth/admin_handlers.go` (lines 1-430)
- **Admin Uploads Handler**: `cmd/unified-server/main.go` (line 4970)
- **Admin Tracks Handler**: `cmd/unified-server/main.go` (line 6186)

### 4. **Admin Authentication Pattern**

Two-layer authentication approach:

**Layer 1: Middleware** (`pkg/auth/middleware.go`)
- `OptionalAuth()` - Attempts to authenticate via session, doesn't fail if not authenticated
- `RequireAuth()` - Requires session authentication
- Both look for user context via `auth.GetUserFromContext()`

**Layer 2: Admin Access Check** (in `register.go` and `main.go`)
- Function: `checkAdminAccess()` (pkg/httpapi/register.go, line 136-145)
- Also: `checkAdminAuth()` (cmd/unified-server/main.go, line 4944)

**Authentication Priority**:
1. **Session-based**: Check if user is logged in via session AND has `is_admin=true`
2. **Fallback**: Check for URL parameter `?password=xxx` matching `adminPassword` flag

**Code Pattern**:
```go
func checkAdminAccess(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, adminPassword string) bool {
    if user, ok := auth.GetUserFromContext(r.Context()); ok && user.IsAdmin {
        return true
    }
    if adminPassword != "" && r.URL.Query().Get("password") == adminPassword {
        return true
    }
    http.Error(w, "Unauthorized - Please login as admin or provide password", http.StatusUnauthorized)
    return false
}
```

### 5. **Navigation/Layout Pattern**

All admin pages use a consistent navigation pattern:

**Top Navigation Bar** (`.nav` class):
- `.nav-left` - Contains navigation links and action buttons
- Navigation links to other admin pages (cross-linking)
- Action buttons (Add, Delete, Backfill, etc.)
- `.back-to-map-btn` - Blue button to return to main map

**Visual Hierarchy**:
```
H1 Title
├── Navigation Bar (.nav)
├── Summary/Status Section
├── Main Content (Table)
└── Pagination Controls
```

**Example Navigation**:
- Uploads page: Links to "All Tracks" and "Tracked Uploads"
- Tracks page: Links to "All Tracks" and "Tracked Uploads"

### 6. **Styling System**

All admin pages use **CSS custom properties** for consistent theming:

**Light Mode Variables**:
```css
--bg-primary: #f5f5f5
--bg-card: white
--text-primary: #333
--text-secondary: #666
--border-color: #ddd
--link-color: #0066cc
--th-bg: #424242
```

**Dark Mode Variables** (via `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` or `[data-theme='dark']`):
```css
--bg-primary: #1a1a1a
--bg-card: #2b2b2b
--text-primary: #eee
--text-secondary: #aaa
--border-color: #444
--link-color: #90caf9
--th-bg: #616161
```

**Reusable Component Classes**:
- `.badge` / `.badge-active` / `.badge-inactive` - Status indicators
- `.btn-primary` / `.btn-secondary` / `.btn-danger` - Button styles
- `.modal` / `.modal-content` / `.modal-header` - Dialog patterns
- `.pagination-btn` - Pagination styling
- `.sortable` - Sortable column headers

### 7. **UI Patterns Already in Use**

**Sortable Tables** (admin-users.html, lines 84-88):
```javascript
.sortable { cursor: pointer; user-select: none; position: relative; padding-right: 20px; }
.sortable::after { content: '⇅'; position: absolute; right: 8px; opacity: 0.5; }
.sortable.asc::after { content: '▲'; opacity: 1; }
.sortable.desc::after { content: '▼'; opacity: 1; }
```

**Client-Side Filtering** (lines 608-631):
```javascript
function filterTable() {
    // Filters applied per column via input fields
}
```

**Modal Dialogs** (lines 112-309):
```html
.modal { display: none; position: fixed; z-index: 1000; ... }
<!-- Modal content structure -->
```

**Pagination Controls** (lines 138-150):
- First/Prev/Next/Last buttons
- Page number buttons with ellipsis
- Page size selector (25, 50, 100, 200 items)

**Search/Filter Bar** (lines 180-186):
```html
<input type="text" id="searchInput" placeholder="Search email, username, ID...">
<button class="search-btn" onclick="doSearch()">Search</button>
```

**No Tabbed UI Currently**: 
- No native tab components found in existing admin pages
- All pages use full-page layout with cross-page navigation instead

### 8. **Data Flow for Admin Users**

**Frontend** (admin-users.html):
1. User navigates to `/admin/users` with optional `?password=xxx`
2. HTML page loads and checks authentication on DOMContentLoaded
3. Calls `/api/admin/users?limit=50&offset=0` to fetch user list
4. Displays paginated table with search/filter
5. User interactions (add, edit, delete) call respective API endpoints

**Backend** (pkg/auth/admin_handlers.go):
1. Request arrives at authenticated route
2. `checkAdminAccess()` verifies session or password
3. Handler function processes request:
   - `AdminListUsersHandler()` - Returns JSON list with pagination
   - `AdminCreateUserHandler()` - Creates user, sends welcome email if configured
   - `AdminUpdateUserHandler()` - Updates fields, handles optional password
   - `AdminDeleteUserHandler()` - Soft or hard delete
   - `AdminResetUserPasswordHandler()` - Creates reset token, sends email

### 9. **Key Technical Details**

**Authentication Context** (`pkg/auth/middleware.go`):
- User stored in request context via `WithAuthContext()`
- Retrieved with `GetUserFromContext()`
- User model includes: `ID`, `Email`, `Username`, `IsAdmin`, `IsActive`, `EmailVerified`, `APIKey`, etc.

**Database Operations** (`pkg/auth/database.go`):
- `GetUsersPaginated()` - Fetch paginated list
- `CountUsers()` - Get total count for pagination
- `CreateUser()` - Insert new user with hashed password
- `UpdateUser()` - Update user record
- `DeleteUser()` - Remove user from database

**Password Reset Flow**:
1. Admin triggers reset via "Reset Password" button
2. Backend generates token, stores in `password_reset_tokens` table
3. Email sent with reset link (7-day expiry for admin-initiated, 1-hour default)
4. User clicks link to `/reset-password?token=xxx`
5. User sets new password, token marked as used

### 10. **File Locations Summary**

| Component | Location |
|-----------|----------|
| Admin Users HTML | `cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-users.html` |
| Admin Handlers (Users) | `pkg/auth/admin_handlers.go` |
| Route Registration | `pkg/httpapi/register.go` |
| Page Serving | `cmd/unified-server/main.go` (lines 8828-8872) |
| Uploads Handler | `cmd/unified-server/main.go` (lines 4970-5755) |
| Tracks Handler | `cmd/unified-server/main.go` (lines 6186-6810) |
| Middleware | `pkg/auth/middleware.go` |
| Auth Context | `pkg/auth/auth.go` |
| User Models | `pkg/auth/models.go` |

This comprehensive structure provides a solid foundation for extending admin functionality. The dual authentication pattern (session + password) is flexible, and the CSS theming system makes styling consistent and maintainable across all admin pages.

## Comprehensive Analysis: MCP Server Analytics and DuckLake Data Structures

I've completed a thorough exploration of the MCP server analytics and DuckLake implementation. Here's the complete picture:

### 1. DuckLake Tables: Schemas and DDL

All three analytics tables are created identically in both MCP server and unified server during initialization:

#### **mcp_query_log** (Tool execution usage statistics)
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_query_log (
    tool_name VARCHAR,
    timestamp TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    duration_ms BIGINT,
    result_count INTEGER,
    client VARCHAR,
    user_id VARCHAR,
    user_email VARCHAR
)
```

**Source files:**
- `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/duckdb_client.go` (lines 85-92)
- `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/duckdb_analytics.go` (lines 112-120)

#### **mcp_ai_query_log** (AI tool execution and query logs)
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_ai_query_log (
    user_id VARCHAR,
    user_email VARCHAR,
    session_id VARCHAR,
    timestamp TIMESTAMP,
    tool_name VARCHAR,
    generated_query VARCHAR,
    duration_ms BIGINT,
    commit_hash VARCHAR,
    error VARCHAR
)
```

**Source files:**
- `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/duckdb_client.go` (lines 93-103)
- `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/duckdb_analytics.go` (lines 121-131)

#### **chat_questions** (Web-chat and map widget questions with full metadata)
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chat_questions (
    id BIGINT,
    timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
    question VARCHAR,
    source VARCHAR,
    ip_address VARCHAR,
    user_agent VARCHAR,
    is_mobile BOOLEAN,
    os VARCHAR,
    browser VARCHAR,
    country VARCHAR,
    accept_language VARCHAR,
    referer VARCHAR,
    session_id VARCHAR,
    history_length INTEGER,
    model VARCHAR,
    cloudfront BOOLEAN,
    client_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ,
    answer VARCHAR
)
```

**Source files:**
- `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/duckdb_client.go` (lines 104-123)
- `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/duckdb_analytics.go` (lines 132-151)

---

### 2. How Analytics Data is Written

#### **MCP Query Log (tool usage)**

**MCP Server:**
- Location: `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/duckdb_client.go` (lines 136-159)
- Function: `LogQueryAsync(toolName string, params map[string]any, resultCount int, duration time.Duration, clientInfo string)`
- Called from: `instrument()` wrapper in `main.go` (line 74)
- Inserts asynchronously via goroutine
- Parameters JSON-encoded and stored in params field

**Unified Server:**
- Location: `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/mcp_db_helpers.go` (lines 67-94)
- Function: `LogQueryAsync(name string, args map[string]any, resultCount int, duration time.Duration, client string)`
- Called from: `instrumentMCP()` wrapper in `mcp_register.go` (line 510)
- Extracts user_id and user_email from tool arguments
- Async insertion with silent error handling

#### **AI Query Log (tool execution with query details)**

**MCP Server:**
- Location: `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/ai_logging.go` (lines 34-82, 85-130)
- Function: `logAISessionWithUser(toolName, query, duration, err, userID, userEmail)`
- Async logging to DuckDB table `mcp_ai_query_log`
- Sanitizes query (collapses whitespace, removes string literals)
- Truncates queries to 1000 chars
- Generates RFC4122 v4 UUIDs for session IDs
- Includes git commit hash (cached on first call)

**Unified Server:**
- Location: `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/ai_logging.go` (identical code)
- Called from: `instrumentMCP()` in `mcp_register.go` (lines 512-519)

**Key data collection:**
- `user_id`, `user_email`: Extracted from MCP request arguments
- `session_id`: Generated random UUID v4
- `timestamp`: RFC3339 format (UTC)
- `generated_query`: Sanitized SQL/query string
- `duration_ms`: Milliseconds
- `commit_hash`: Git HEAD revision
- `error`: Error message (if any)

#### **Chat Questions (web-chat analytics)**

**Location:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/chat_logging.go` (lines 14-78)

**Function:** `logChatQuestion(r *http.Request, question, source, model, sessionID string, historyLen int, clientTimestamp string) int64`
- Called from: `handleWebChat()` in `mcp_register.go` (line 209)
- Inserts question record and returns auto-generated ID
- Uses `time.Now().UnixNano()` for unique ID (DuckLake has no RETURNING or sequences)
- Extracts metadata from HTTP request headers:
  - IP address (respects X-Forwarded-For, X-Real-IP proxies)
  - User-Agent header (parses Mobile/Desktop, OS, Browser)
  - CloudFront headers detection
  - CloudFront-Viewer-Country
  - Accept-Language
  - Referer
- Caps question at 5000 chars

**Answer logging:** `logChatAnswer(rowID int64, answer string)`
- Updates the same row asynchronously with AI response
- Async to avoid blocking chat response
- Caps answer at 50,000 chars

---

### 3. How Analytics Data is Currently Queried/Read

#### **MCP Tools (Query-exposed analytics)**

**query_analytics MCP Tool:**
- **Files:** 
  - `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/tool_analytics.go` (lines 12-68)
  - `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/tool_analytics.go` (identical)
- **Description:** "Get usage statistics for MCP tools (call counts, duration). Powered by DuckDB local logs."
- **Query:**
  ```sql
  SELECT tool_name, COUNT(*) as count,
         AVG(duration_ms) as avg_ms,
         MAX(duration_ms) as max_ms
  FROM mcp_query_log
  GROUP BY tool_name
  ORDER BY count DESC
  ```
- **Returns:** Tool name, call count, average duration, max duration
- **Registration:** Added to MCP server via `mcpServer.AddTool(queryAnalyticsToolDef, ...)`

**radiation_stats MCP Tool:**
- **Files:** Same as above (lines 16-161)
- **Supports three aggregation intervals:** year, month, overall
- **Queries attached PostgreSQL database** via DuckDB proxy
- **Example (year interval):**
  ```sql
  SELECT
    EXTRACT(YEAR FROM to_timestamp(date)::TIMESTAMP) AS year,
    COUNT(*) AS count,
    AVG(doserate) AS avg_value,
    MAX(doserate) AS max_value
  FROM postgres_db.public.markers
  WHERE doserate > 0 AND doserate < 1000
  GROUP BY 1
  ORDER BY 1 DESC
  LIMIT 20
  ```

**query_duckdb_logs MCP Tool:**
- **Files:** 
  - `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/tool_duckdb_logs.go` (lines 11-86)
  - `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/tool_duckdb_logs.go` (identical)
- **Description:** "Query MCP AI logs stored in DuckDB. Supports simple SQL SELECT queries. Available tables: mcp_ai_query_log, mcp_query_log, chat_questions..."
- **Purpose:** Generic SQL interface for custom analytics queries
- **Restrictions:** SELECT only (no modifications)
- **Returns:** Text-formatted query results (one row per line)

#### **REST API Endpoints**

**GET /api/stats** (Unified server):
- **File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/rest_stats.go` (lines 1-44)
- **Function:** `handleStats(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request)`
- **Query params:** `interval` (year, month, overall) - defaults to year
- **Backend:** Reuses `handleRadiationStats()` MCP tool
- **Returns:** JSON with interval, data array, source, AI hints, generated note

#### **Admin Pages**

**Admin Uploads:**
- **File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/main.go`
- **Route:** `/admin/uploads` or `/api/admin/uploads`
- **Auth:** URL password (`?password=xxx`) or session-based admin auth
- **Data:** Lists file uploads with metadata and search functionality

**Admin Tracks:**
- **Route:** `/api/admin/tracks`
- **Auth:** URL password or session-based admin auth
- **Data:** Lists all tracks with statistics

### 4. DuckDB/DuckLake Connection Setup Code

#### **MCP Server Setup**

**File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/mcp-server/duckdb_client.go` (lines 16-134)

**Function:** `initDuckDB() error`

**Steps:**
1. Opens in-memory DuckDB: `sql.Open("duckdb", "")`
2. Sets connection pool (1 max open, 1 idle, no lifetime limit)
3. Installs and loads extensions: `ducklake`, `postgres`
4. Attaches DuckLake catalog via PostgreSQL:
   ```go
   ATTACH 'ducklake:postgres:{DUCKLAKE_PG_URL}' AS analytics (DATA_PATH '{DUCKLAKE_DATA_PATH}');
   USE analytics;
   ```
5. Attaches main PostgreSQL database (read-only):
   ```go
   ATTACH '{DATABASE_URL}' AS postgres_db (TYPE POSTGRES, READ_ONLY);
   ```
6. Creates three analytics tables (if not exist)

**Environment Variables:**
- `DUCKLAKE_PG_URL` - PostgreSQL catalog connection (default: `dbname=ducklake_catalog host=localhost user=ducklake_rw`)
- `DUCKLAKE_DATA_PATH` - Parquet data directory (default: `/var/lib/safecast/ducklake/`)
- `DATABASE_URL` - Main Safecast PostgreSQL connection

**Error Handling:** Logs warnings but continues; analytics features degrade gracefully if DuckDB fails

#### **Unified Server Setup**

**File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/duckdb_analytics.go` (lines 18-162)

**Function:** `initDuckDBAnalytics() error`

**Key differences:**
- Helper function `attachPostgres()` for more flexible PostgreSQL attachment
- Slightly different table schema for `mcp_query_log` (no timestamp default, different field order)
- Called from `RegisterMCP()` in `mcp_register.go` during server startup

#### **Global Variable**

Both servers use:
```go
var duckDB *sql.DB
```

Shared connection accessed by all analytics functions.

---

### 5. Existing Export Functionality

**Status:** NO DEDICATED EXPORT FUNCTIONALITY EXISTS

The analytics system is currently **query-only**:
- Via MCP tools (`query_duckdb_logs`, `query_analytics`, `radiation_stats`)
- Via REST API (`/api/stats`)
- Via admin pages (uploads, tracks)

**No CSV/JSON export endpoints** are implemented. Query results are:
- Returned as JSON from REST endpoints
- Returned as text-formatted rows from `query_duckdb_logs` MCP tool
- Rendered in HTML admin pages

**Parquet export:** The DuckLake catalog stores data in Parquet format at `DUCKLAKE_DATA_PATH` (`/var/lib/safecast/ducklake/` in production), but there's no exposed mechanism to export from within the application.

---

### Architecture Summary

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Unified Server (8765) + MCP Server (3333)  │
└────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘
                 │
         ┌───────┴────────┐
         │                │
    ┌────▼──────┐    ┌────▼──────┐
    │  DuckDB   │    │ PostgreSQL │
    │ In-Memory │    │  Main DB   │
    └────┬──────┘    └────┬───────┘
         │                │
         │ ATTACH         │ READ_ONLY ATTACH
         │                │
         ▼                ▼
    ┌────────────────────────────────┐
    │  DuckLake (Shared Catalog)     │
    │  - PostgreSQL catalog DB       │
    │  - Parquet data files          │
    │  - Tables:                     │
    │    - chat_questions            │
    │    - mcp_query_log             │
    │    - mcp_ai_query_log          │
    └────────────────────────────────┘
         │
         ├─ Env: DUCKLAKE_PG_URL
         └─ Env: DUCKLAKE_DATA_PATH
            (/var/lib/safecast/ducklake/)
```

**Key Files Summary:**

| Component | MCP Server | Unified Server |
|-----------|-----------|-----------------|
| DuckDB Init | `duckdb_client.go` | `duckdb_analytics.go` |
| AI Logging | `ai_logging.go` | `ai_logging.go` |
| Chat Logging | N/A | `chat_logging.go` |
| Tool Instrumentation | `main.go` | `mcp_register.go` |

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Admin MCP Analytics Page with Tabbed Navigation

## Context
The MCP server and web-chat collect analytics into 3 DuckLake tables (`chat_questions`, `mcp_query_log`, `mcp_ai_query_log`) but there's no admin UI to browse this data. The user wants a new admin page to view/search/export MCP analytics, and to reorganize all admin pages under a tabbed navigation system.

## Approach

### 1. Add tabbed navigation to all admin pages
- Create a shared tab bar component (HTML/CSS snippet) used across all admin pages
- Tabs: **Users** | **Uploads** | **Tracks** | **MCP Analytics** | ~~Realtime~~ (greyed out, future)
- Active tab highlighted based on current page
- Each tab links to its respective `/admin/*` route
- Update existing pages (admin-users.html, uploads handler, tracks handler) to include the tab bar

### 2. Create new MCP Analytics admin page

**New file:** `cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-mcp.html`

Self-contained HTML page (same pattern as admin-users.html) with:

**Sub-tabs** within the MCP page for the 3 tables:
- **Chat Questions** — `chat_questions` table (default view)
- **Tool Usage** — `mcp_query_log` table
- **AI Query Log** — `mcp_ai_query_log` table

**Features per sub-tab:**
- Sortable data table showing ALL fields from each table
- Column filter inputs (like admin-users.html pattern)
- Global search bar
- Pagination (configurable page size: 25/50/100/200)
- **Export button** — CSV download of current filtered/full dataset
- Date range filter (since all tables have timestamps)
- Dark/light theme support (reuse existing CSS variables)

### 3. Backend: New API endpoints

**File:** `cmd/unified-server/main.go` (or new file `cmd/unified-server/admin_mcp.go`)

```
GET /api/admin/mcp/chat-questions?limit=50&offset=0&sort=timestamp&order=desc&search=...
GET /api/admin/mcp/tool-usage?limit=50&offset=0&sort=timestamp&order=desc
GET /api/admin/mcp/ai-queries?limit=50&offset=0&sort=timestamp&order=desc
GET /api/admin/mcp/export?table=chat_questions&format=csv
```

All endpoints:
- Protected by `checkAdminAccess()` (session admin or password)
- Query DuckDB via the existing `duckDB` global connection
- Return JSON with `{data: [...], total: N, limit: N, offset: N}`
- Export endpoint streams CSV with `Content-Disposition: attachment`

### 4. Route registration

**File:** `cmd/unified-server/main.go` (in the admin routes section ~line 8845)

```go
http.HandleFunc("/admin/mcp", authManager.OptionalAuth(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    // Serve admin-mcp.html from embedded content
}))
```

### 5. Files to modify

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-users.html` | Add tab bar navigation at top |
| `cmd/unified-server/main.go` | Add tab bar to uploads/tracks handlers, add `/admin/mcp` route, register API endpoints |
| **New:** `cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-mcp.html` | Full MCP analytics page |
| **New:** `cmd/unified-server/admin_mcp.go` | API handlers for MCP data queries + CSV export |

### 6. Tab bar design

```html
<div class="admin-tabs">
  <a href="/admin/users" class="tab active">Users</a>
  <a href="/admin/uploads" class="tab">Uploads</a>
  <a href="/admin/tracks" class="tab">Tracks</a>
  <a href="/admin/mcp" class="tab">MCP Analytics</a>
  <span class="tab disabled">Realtime</span>
</div>
```

The tab bar will pass the admin password via query param in the href (same pattern as existing cross-page links in uploads/tracks).

## Verification
1. Build: `/usr/local/go/bin/go build -o safecast-new-map ./cmd/unified-server/`
2. Run locally with `bash local-server-config.sh`
3. Visit `/admin/users`, `/admin/uploads`, `/admin/tracks` — verify tab bar appears on all
4. Visit `/admin/mcp` — verify all 3 sub-tabs show data from DuckLake tables
5. Test CSV export downloads
6. Test search/filter/sort/pagination
7. Test dark mode

```

     1→// DuckDB Analytics Initialization for Unified Server
     2→// Uses DuckLake with PostgreSQL catalog for shared analytics across all services
     3→
     4→package main
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"database/sql"
     8→	"fmt"
     9→	"log"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"strings"
    12→
    13→	_ "github.com/duckdb/duckdb-go/v2"
    14→)
    15→
    16→var duckDB *sql.DB
    17→
    18→// initDuckDBAnalytics initializes DuckDB with DuckLake catalog backed by PostgreSQL.
    19→// This allows multiple services to share the same analytics tables concurrently.
    20→func initDuckDBAnalytics() error {
    21→	// Open in-memory DuckDB — all persistent data lives in DuckLake (PostgreSQL + Parquet)
    22→	var err error
    23→	duckDB, err = sql.Open("duckdb", "")
    24→	if err != nil {
    25→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open duckdb: %w", err)
    26→	}
    27→
    28→	duckDB.SetMaxOpenConns(1)
    29→	duckDB.SetMaxIdleConns(1)
    30→	duckDB.SetConnMaxLifetime(0)
    31→
    32→	if err := duckDB.Ping(); err != nil {
    33→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ping duckdb: %w", err)
    34→	}
    35→
    36→	log.Println("DuckDB initialized (in-memory)")
    37→
    38→	// Install and load required extensions
    39→	for _, ext := range []string{"ducklake", "postgres"} {
    40→		if _, err := duckDB.Exec(fmt.Sprintf("INSTALL %s;", ext)); err != nil {
    41→			log.Printf("Warning: INSTALL %s failed: %v", ext, err)
    42→		}
    43→		if _, err := duckDB.Exec(fmt.Sprintf("LOAD %s;", ext)); err != nil {
    44→			return fmt.Errorf("LOAD %s: %w", ext, err)
    45→		}
    46→	}
    47→
    48→	// Attach DuckLake catalog via PostgreSQL
    49→	ducklakePGURL := os.Getenv("DUCKLAKE_PG_URL")
    50→	if ducklakePGURL == "" {
    51→		ducklakePGURL = "dbname=ducklake_catalog host=localhost user=ducklake_rw"
    52→	}
    53→	dataPath := os.Getenv("DUCKLAKE_DATA_PATH")
    54→	if dataPath == "" {
    55→		dataPath = "/var/lib/safecast/ducklake/"
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	attachQuery := fmt.Sprintf(
    59→		"ATTACH 'ducklake:postgres:%s' AS analytics (DATA_PATH '%s');",
    60→		ducklakePGURL, dataPath,
    61→	)
    62→	if _, err := duckDB.Exec(attachQuery); err != nil {
    63→		return fmt.Errorf("attach DuckLake: %w", err)
    64→	}
    65→	log.Printf("DuckLake attached (catalog=PostgreSQL, data=%s)", dataPath)
    66→
    67→	// Use analytics as default database
    68→	if _, err := duckDB.Exec("USE analytics;"); err != nil {
    69→		return fmt.Errorf("USE analytics: %w", err)
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	// Also attach main Safecast PostgreSQL for cross-database queries (read-only)
    73→	databaseURL := os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL")
    74→	if databaseURL == "" {
    75→		// Unified server uses -db-conn flag; construct URL from that
    76→		// Fall back to environment variable
    77→	}
    78→	if databaseURL != "" {
    79→		if err := attachPostgres(databaseURL); err != nil {
    80→			log.Printf("Warning: PostgreSQL attach failed: %v (cross-db analytics disabled)", err)
    81→		} else {
    82→			log.Println("Safecast PostgreSQL attached for cross-database queries")
    83→		}
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	// Create analytics schema in DuckLake
    87→	if err := createDuckDBSchema(); err != nil {
    88→		log.Printf("Warning: failed to create DuckLake schema: %v", err)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	return nil
    92→}
    93→
    94→// attachPostgres attaches the main Safecast PostgreSQL for read-only cross-database queries
    95→func attachPostgres(databaseURL string) error {
    96→	attachStr := databaseURL
    97→	if !strings.Contains(databaseURL, "?") {
    98→		attachStr = databaseURL + "?sslmode=prefer"
    99→	}
   100→
   101→	query := fmt.Sprintf("ATTACH '%s' AS postgres_db (TYPE POSTGRES, READ_ONLY);", attachStr)
   102→	if _, err := duckDB.Exec(query); err != nil {
   103→		return fmt.Errorf("attach postgres: %w", err)
   104→	}
   105→	return nil
   106→}
   107→
   108→// createDuckDBSchema creates the shared analytics tables in DuckLake
   109→func createDuckDBSchema() error {
   110→	// DuckLake doesn't support multi-statement exec, so run each separately
   111→	tables := []string{
   112→		`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_query_log (
   113→			tool_name VARCHAR,
   114→			timestamp TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
   115→			duration_ms BIGINT,
   116→			result_count INTEGER,
   117→			client VARCHAR,
   118→			user_id VARCHAR,
   119→			user_email VARCHAR
   120→		)`,
   121→		`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS mcp_ai_query_log (
   122→			user_id VARCHAR,
   123→			user_email VARCHAR,
   124→			session_id VARCHAR,
   125→			timestamp TIMESTAMP,
   126→			tool_name VARCHAR,
   127→			generated_query VARCHAR,
   128→			duration_ms BIGINT,
   129→			commit_hash VARCHAR,
   130→			error VARCHAR
   131→		)`,
   132→		`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chat_questions (
   133→			id BIGINT,
   134→			timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now(),
   135→			question VARCHAR,
   136→			source VARCHAR,
   137→			ip_address VARCHAR,
   138→			user_agent VARCHAR,
   139→			is_mobile BOOLEAN,
   140→			os VARCHAR,
   141→			browser VARCHAR,
   142→			country VARCHAR,
   143→			accept_language VARCHAR,
   144→			referer VARCHAR,
   145→			session_id VARCHAR,
   146→			history_length INTEGER,
   147→			model VARCHAR,
   148→			cloudfront BOOLEAN,
   149→			client_timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ,
   150→			answer VARCHAR
   151→		)`,
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	for _, ddl := range tables {
   155→		if _, err := duckDB.Exec(ddl); err != nil {
   156→			return fmt.Errorf("create schema: %w", err)
   157→		}
   158→	}
   159→
   160→	log.Println("DuckLake analytics schema ready")
   161→	return nil
   162→}
   163→
   164→// duckDBAvailable returns true if DuckDB is initialized
   165→func duckDBAvailable() bool {
   166→	return duckDB != nil
   167→}
   168→
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/admin_mcp.go",
  "content": "package main\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/csv\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"strconv\"\n\t\"strings\"\n)\n\n// adminMCPDataHandler returns JSON data for MCP analytics tables.\n// GET /api/admin/mcp/data?table=chat_questions&limit=50&offset=0&sort=timestamp&order=desc&search=...\nfunc adminMCPDataHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Analytics not available\", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\ttableName := r.URL.Query().Get(\"table\")\n\tcolumns, ok := mcpTableColumns[tableName]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Invalid table name\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tlimit, _ := strconv.Atoi(r.URL.Query().Get(\"limit\"))\n\tif limit <= 0 || limit > 1000 {\n\t\tlimit = 50\n\t}\n\toffset, _ := strconv.Atoi(r.URL.Query().Get(\"offset\"))\n\tif offset < 0 {\n\t\toffset = 0\n\t}\n\n\tsortCol := r.URL.Query().Get(\"sort\")\n\tif !isValidColumn(sortCol, columns) {\n\t\tsortCol = \"timestamp\"\n\t}\n\torder := strings.ToUpper(r.URL.Query().Get(\"order\"))\n\tif order != \"ASC\" {\n\t\torder = \"DESC\"\n\t}\n\n\tsearch := r.URL.Query().Get(\"search\")\n\n\t// Build WHERE clause for search\n\tvar whereClauses []string\n\tif search != \"\" {\n\t\tfor _, col := range columns {\n\t\t\twhereClauses = append(whereClauses, fmt.Sprintf(\"CAST(%s AS VARCHAR) ILIKE '%%%s%%'\", col, escapeLike(search)))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\twhereSQL := \"\"\n\tif len(whereClauses) > 0 {\n\t\twhereSQL = \"WHERE \" + strings.Join(whereClauses, \" OR \")\n\t}\n\n\t// Count total\n\tcountQuery := fmt.Sprintf(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM %s %s\", tableName, whereSQL)\n\tvar total int\n\tif err := duckDB.QueryRow(countQuery).Scan(&total); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin mcp count error: %v\", err)\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Query failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Fetch data\n\tcolList := strings.Join(columns, \", \")\n\tdataQuery := fmt.Sprintf(\"SELECT %s FROM %s %s ORDER BY %s %s LIMIT %d OFFSET %d\",\n\t\tcolList, tableName, whereSQL, sortCol, order, limit, offset)\n\n\trows, err := duckDB.Query(dataQuery)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin mcp query error: %v\", err)\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Query failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar results []map[string]interface{}\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvalues := make([]interface{}, len(columns))\n\t\tptrs := make([]interface{}, len(columns))\n\t\tfor i := range values {\n\t\t\tptrs[i] = &values[i]\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(ptrs...); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin mcp scan error: %v\", err)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\trow := make(map[string]interface{})\n\t\tfor i, col := range columns {\n\t\t\trow[col] = values[i]\n\t\t}\n\t\tresults = append(results, row)\n\t}\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\"data\":    results,\n\t\t\"total\":   total,\n\t\t\"limit\":   limit,\n\t\t\"offset\":  offset,\n\t\t\"columns\": columns,\n\t})\n}\n\n// adminMCPExportHandler exports MCP analytics data as CSV.\n// GET /api/admin/mcp/export?table=chat_questions&search=...\nfunc adminMCPExportHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Analytics not available\", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\ttableName := r.URL.Query().Get(\"table\")\n\tcolumns, ok := mcpTableColumns[tableName]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Invalid table name\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tsearch := r.URL.Query().Get(\"search\")\n\n\tvar whereClauses []string\n\tif search != \"\" {\n\t\tfor _, col := range columns {\n\t\t\twhereClauses = append(whereClauses, fmt.Sprintf(\"CAST(%s AS VARCHAR) ILIKE '%%%s%%'\", col, escapeLike(search)))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\twhereSQL := \"\"\n\tif len(whereClauses) > 0 {\n\t\twhereSQL = \"WHERE \" + strings.Join(whereClauses, \" OR \")\n\t}\n\n\tcolList := strings.Join(columns, \", \")\n\tquery := fmt.Sprintf(\"SELECT %s FROM %s %s ORDER BY timestamp DESC\", colList, tableName, whereSQL)\n\n\trows, err := duckDB.Query(query)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin mcp export error: %v\", err)\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Query failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"text/csv\")\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Disposition\", fmt.Sprintf(\"attachment; filename=%s.csv\", tableName))\n\n\twriter := csv.NewWriter(w)\n\twriter.Write(columns) // header row\n\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvalues := make([]interface{}, len(columns))\n\t\tptrs := make([]interface{}, len(columns))\n\t\tfor i := range values {\n\t\t\tptrs[i] = &values[i]\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(ptrs...); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\trecord := make([]string, len(columns))\n\t\tfor i, v := range values {\n\t\t\tif v == nil {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = \"\"\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = fmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", v)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\twriter.Write(record)\n\t}\n\twriter.Flush()\n}\n\n// mcpTableColumns defines the valid tables and their columns for the admin MCP page.\nvar mcpTableColumns = map[string][]string{\n\t\"chat_questions\": {\n\t\t\"id\", \"timestamp\", \"question\", \"answer\", \"source\", \"model\",\n\t\t\"ip_address\", \"country\", \"is_mobile\", \"os\", \"browser\",\n\t\t\"user_agent\", \"accept_language\", \"referer\",\n\t\t\"session_id\", \"history_length\", \"cloudfront\", \"client_timestamp\",\n\t},\n\t\"mcp_query_log\": {\n\t\t\"tool_name\", \"timestamp\", \"duration_ms\", \"result_count\",\n\t\t\"client\", \"user_id\", \"user_email\",\n\t},\n\t\"mcp_ai_query_log\": {\n\t\t\"user_id\", \"user_email\", \"session_id\", \"timestamp\",\n\t\t\"tool_name\", \"generated_query\", \"duration_ms\",\n\t\t\"commit_hash\", \"error\",\n\t},\n}\n\nfunc isValidColumn(col string, validCols []string) bool {\n\tfor _, c := range validCols {\n\t\tif c == col {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc escapeLike(s string) string {\n\ts = strings.ReplaceAll(s, \"'\", \"''\")\n\ts = strings.ReplaceAll(s, \"%\", \"\")\n\ts = strings.ReplaceAll(s, \"_\", \"\")\n\treturn s\n}\n"
}

}\n\n    /* Summary */\n    .summary { margin-bottom: 10px; color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 0.9em; }\n\n    /* Data table */\n    .table-wrap { overflow-x: auto; background: var(--bg-card); border-radius: 8px; word-break: break-word; }\n    tr:hover { background: var(--hover-bg); }\n    .sortable { cursor: pointer; user-select: none; position: relative; padding-right: 20px; }\n    .sortable:hover { background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1); }\n    .sortable::after { content: '\\21C5'; position: absolute; right: 4px; opacity: 0.5; }\n    .sortable.asc::after { content: '\\25B2'; opacity: 1; }\n    .sortable.desc::after { content: '\\25BC'; opacity: 1; }\n\n    /* Expandable cell */\n    td.expandable { cursor: pointer; }\n    td.expandable:hover { white-space: normal; overflow: visible; position: relative; z-index: 10; background: var(--bg-card); box-shadow: var(--shadow); }\n\n    /* Pagination */\n    .pagination { display: flex; gap: 5px; align-items: center; justify-content: center; margin-top: 15px; flex-wrap: wrap; }\n    .pagination button { padding: 6px 12px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; background: var(--bg-card); color: var(--text-primary); cursor: pointer; }\n    .pagination button:hover { background: var(--hover-bg); }\n    .pagination button.active { background: var(--tab-active-bg); color: white; border-color: var(--tab-active-bg); }\n    .pagination button:disabled { opacity: 0.4; cursor: not-allowed; }\n    .pagination select { padding: 6px 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border-color); border-radius: 4px; background: var(--bg-card); color: var(--text-primary); }\n\n    .no-data { text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: var(--text-muted); }\n    .loading { text-align: center; padding: 40px; color: var(--text-muted); }\n\n    /* Modal for viewing full content */\n    .modal-overlay { display: none; position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5); z-index: 1000; }\n    .modal-overlay.visible { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; }\n    .modal { background: var(--bg-card); border-radius: 12px; padding: 20px; max-width: 800px; width: 90%; max-height: 80vh; overflow-y: auto; box-shadow: 0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,0.3); }\n    .modal h3 { margin-bottom: 10px; }\n    .modal pre { white-space: pre-wrap; word-break: break-word; font-size: 0.9em; line-height: 1.5; background: var(--bg-primary); padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px; max-height: 60vh; overflow-y: auto; }\n    .modal button { margin-top: 15px; padding: 8px 20px; background: var(--tab-active-bg); color: white; border: none; border-radius: var(--btn-border-radius); cursor: pointer; }\n  </style>\n</head>\n<body>\n\n<h1>MCP Analytics</h1>\n\n<div class=\"admin-tabs\" id=\"adminTabs\"></div>\n\n<div class=\"sub-tabs\">\n  <button class=\"active\" onclick=\"switchTable('chat_questions')\">Chat Questions</button>\n  <button onclick=\"switchTable('mcp_query_log')\">Tool Usage</button>\n  <button onclick=\"switchTable('mcp_ai_query_log')\">AI Query Log</button>\n</div>\n\n<div class=\"controls\">\n  <input type=\"text\" id=\"searchInput\" placeholder=\"Search across all fields...\" onkeydown=\"if(event.key==='Enter')doSearch()\">\n  <button onclick=\"doSearch()\">Search</button>\n  <button onclick=\"clearSearch()\">Clear</button>\n  <button class=\"export-btn\" onclick=\"exportCSV()\">Export CSV</button>\n  <span class=\"summary\" id=\"summary\"></span>\n</div>\n\n<div class=\"table-wrap\">\n  <div class=\"loading\" id=\"loading\">Loading...</div>\n  <table id=\"dataTable\" style=\"display:none\">\n    <thead id=\"tableHead\"></thead>\n    <tbody id=\"tableBody\"></tbody>\n  </table>\n  <div class=\"no-data\" id=\"noData\" style=\"display:none\">No data found</div>\n</div>\n\n<div class=\"pagination\" id=\"pagination\"></div>\n\n<div class=\"modal-overlay\" id=\"modalOverlay\" onclick=\"closeModal()\">\n  <div class=\"modal\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation()\">\n    <h3 id=\"modalTitle\">Details</h3>\n    <pre id=\"modalContent\"></pre>\n    <button onclick=\"closeModal()\">Close</button>\n  </div>\n</div>\n\n<script>\nconst PASSWORD = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('password') || '';\nconst AUTH_PARAM = PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '';\n\n// Column display names\nconst COLUMN_LABELS = {\n  id: 'ID', timestamp: 'Timestamp', question: 'Question', answer: 'Answer',\n  source: 'Source', model: 'Model', ip_address: 'IP', country: 'Country',\n  is_mobile: 'Mobile', os: 'OS', browser: 'Browser', user_agent: 'User Agent',\n  accept_language: 'Language', referer: 'Referer', session_id: 'Session',\n  history_length: 'History', cloudfront: 'CF', client_timestamp: 'Client Time',\n  tool_name: 'Tool', duration_ms: 'Duration (ms)', result_count: 'Results',\n  client: 'Client', user_id: 'User ID', user_email: 'Email',\n  generated_query: 'Query', commit_hash: 'Commit', error: 'Error'\n};\n\n// Columns that should be expandable (long text)\nconst EXPANDABLE = new Set(['question', 'answer', 'generated_query', 'user_agent', 'accept_language', 'referer']);\n\nlet currentTable = 'chat_questions';\nlet currentSort = 'timestamp';\nlet currentOrder = 'desc';\nlet currentSearch = '';\nlet currentOffset = 0;\nlet currentLimit = 50;\nlet totalRows = 0;\n\n// Build admin tabs\nfunction buildAdminTabs() {\n  const tabs = [\n    { label: 'Users', href: '/admin/users' },\n    { label: 'Uploads', href: '/admin/uploads' },\n    { label: 'Tracks', href: '/api/admin/tracks' },\n    { label: 'MCP Analytics', href: '/admin/mcp', active: true },\n    { label: 'Realtime', disabled: true }\n  ];\n  const container = document.getElementById('adminTabs');\n  tabs.forEach(t => {\n    if (t.disabled) {\n      container.innerHTML += `<span class=\"disabled\">${t.label}</span>`;\n    } else {\n      const href = t.href + (PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '');\n      container.innerHTML += `<a href=\"${href}\" class=\"${t.active ? 'active' : ''}\">${t.label}</a>`;\n    }\n  });\n}\n\nfunction switchTable(table) {\n  currentTable = table;\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  currentSort = 'timestamp';\n  currentOrder = 'desc';\n\n  // Update sub-tab active state\n  document.querySelectorAll('.sub-tabs button').forEach(btn => {\n    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.textContent === {\n      'chat_questions': 'Chat Questions',\n      'mcp_query_log': 'Tool Usage',\n      'mcp_ai_query_log': 'AI Query Log'\n    }[table]);\n  });\n\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction doSearch() {\n  currentSearch = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.trim();\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction clearSearch() {\n  document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';\n  currentSearch = '';\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction exportCSV() {\n  let url = '/api/admin/mcp/export?table=' + currentTable;\n  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);\n  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);\n  window.location.href = url;\n}\n\nfunction sortBy(col) {\n  if (currentSort === col) {\n    currentOrder = currentOrder === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';\n  } else {\n    currentSort = col;\n    currentOrder = 'desc';\n  }\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nasync function fetchData() {\n  const loading = document.getElementById('loading');\n  const table = document.getElementById('dataTable');\n  const noData = document.getElementById('noData');\n\n  loading.style.display = 'block';\n  table.style.display = 'none';\n  noData.style.display = 'none';\n\n  let url = `/api/admin/mcp/data?table=${currentTable}&limit=${currentLimit}&offset=${currentOffset}&sort=${currentSort}&order=${currentOrder}`;\n  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);\n  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);\n\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch(url);\n    if (!resp.ok) {\n      if (resp.status === 401) {\n        loading.textContent = 'Unauthorized - please login as admin or provide password';\n        return;\n      }\n      throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);\n    }\n\n    const json = await resp.json();\n    totalRows = json.total;\n    const columns = json.columns || [];\n    const data = json.data || [];\n\n    loading.style.display = 'none';\n\n    if (data.length === 0) {\n      noData.style.display = 'block';\n      document.getElementById('summary').textContent = 'No results';\n      document.getElementById('pagination').innerHTML = '';\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // Build header\n    const thead = document.getElementById('tableHead');\n    let headerHTML = '<tr>';\n    columns.forEach(col => {\n      const label = COLUMN_LABELS[col] || col;\n      const sortClass = currentSort === col ? (currentOrder === 'asc' ? 'sortable asc' : 'sortable desc') : 'sortable';\n      headerHTML += `<th class=\"${sortClass}\" onclick=\"sortBy('${col}')\">${label}</th>`;\n    });\n    headerHTML += '</tr>';\n    thead.innerHTML = headerHTML;\n\n    // Build body\n    const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');\n    let bodyHTML = '';\n    data.forEach(row => {\n      bodyHTML += '<tr>';\n      columns.forEach(col => {\n        let val = row[col];\n        if (val === null || val === undefined) val = '';\n        const strVal = String(val);\n        if (EXPANDABLE.has(col) && strVal.length > 60) {\n          const preview = strVal.substring(0, 60) + '...';\n          bodyHTML += `<td class=\"expandable\" title=\"Click to expand\" onclick=\"showModal('${COLUMN_LABELS[col] || col}', ${JSON.stringify(strVal).replace(/'/g, \"&#39;\")})\">${escapeHtml(preview)}</td>`;\n        } else if (col === 'is_mobile' || col === 'cloudfront') {\n          bodyHTML += `<td>${val ? 'Yes' : (val === false ? 'No' : '')}</td>`;\n        } else if (col === 'timestamp' || col === 'client_timestamp') {\n          bodyHTML += `<td>${formatTimestamp(strVal)}</td>`;\n        } else {\n          bodyHTML += `<td>${escapeHtml(strVal)}</td>`;\n        }\n      });\n      bodyHTML += '</tr>';\n    });\n    tbody.innerHTML = bodyHTML;\n    table.style.display = 'table';\n\n    // Summary\n    const page = Math.floor(currentOffset / currentLimit) + 1;\n    const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRows / currentLimit);\n    document.getElementById('summary').textContent = `${totalRows} total rows | Page ${page} of ${totalPages}`;\n\n    // Pagination\n    buildPagination(totalPages, page);\n\n  } catch (err) {\n    loading.textContent = 'Error loading data: ' + err.message;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction buildPagination(totalPages, currentPage) {\n  const container = document.getElementById('pagination');\n  let html = '';\n\n  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(1)\">First</button>`;\n  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(${currentPage - 1})\">Prev</button>`;\n\n  // Page number buttons (show max 7)\n  let start = Math.max(1, currentPage - 3);\n  let end = Math.min(totalPages, start + 6);\n  start = Math.max(1, end - 6);\n\n  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {\n    html += `<button class=\"${i === currentPage ? 'active' : ''}\" onclick=\"goToPage(${i})\">${i}</button>`;\n  }\n\n  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(${currentPage + 1})\">Next</button>`;\n  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(${totalPages})\">Last</button>`;\n\n  html += ` <select onchange=\"changeLimit(this.value)\">`;\n  [25, 50, 100, 200].forEach(n => {\n    html += `<option value=\"${n}\" ${n === currentLimit ? 'selected' : ''}>${n} per page</option>`;\n  });\n  html += `</select>`;\n\n  container.innerHTML = html;\n}\n\nfunction goToPage(page) {\n  currentOffset = (page - 1) * currentLimit;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction changeLimit(val) {\n  currentLimit = parseInt(val);\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction showModal(title, content) {\n  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = title;\n  document.getElementById('modalContent').textContent = content;\n  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.add('visible');\n}\n\nfunction closeModal() {\n  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.remove('visible');\n}\n\nfunction escapeHtml(str) {\n  const div = document.createElement('div');\n  div.textContent = str;\n  return div.innerHTML;\n}\n\nfunction formatTimestamp(ts) {\n  if (!ts) return '';\n  try {\n    const d = new Date(ts);\n    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return ts;\n    return d.toLocaleString();\n  } catch { return ts; }\n}\n\n// Init\ndocument.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {\n  buildAdminTabs();\n  fetchData();\n});\n\n// Keyboard shortcut: Escape closes modal\ndocument.addEventListener('keydown', e => {\n  if (e.key === 'Escape') closeModal();\n});\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}

> AGENT

Everything builds and passes vet cleanly. Here's a summary of what was done:

**New files:**
- [admin_mcp.go](cmd/unified-server/admin_mcp.go) — Go backend with two handlers:
  - `GET /api/admin/mcp/data?table=...&limit=&offset=&sort=&order=&search=` — Returns paginated JSON data from any of the 3 DuckLake tables
  - `GET /api/admin/mcp/export?table=...&search=` — Streams CSV download
- [admin-mcp.html](cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-mcp.html) — Full MCP analytics page with sub-tabs for Chat Questions / Tool Usage / AI Query Log, sortable columns, search, pagination, CSV export, and click-to-expand for long text fields

**Modified files:**
- [admin-users.html](cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-users.html) — Added admin tab bar at top
- [main.go](cmd/unified-server/main.go) — Added tab bar to uploads/tracks pages, registered `/admin/mcp` route and API endpoints

**Tab navigation** across all admin pages: **Users | Uploads | Tracks | MCP Analytics | Realtime** (greyed out placeholder)

To test locally, rebuild and run with `bash local-server-config.sh`, then visit `/admin/mcp?password=test123`.

> AGENT

Build successful. To get it working you need to:

1. Create the data directory (needs sudo):
   ```bash
   sudo mkdir -p /var/lib/safecast/ducklake/
   sudo chmod 777 /var/lib/safecast/ducklake/
   ```

2. Restart the server:
   ```bash
   bash local-server-config.sh
   ```

The DuckLake catalog DB and user are already created in PostgreSQL. The 500 error was because the DuckLake data directory `/var/lib/safecast/ducklake/` doesn't exist yet on your local machine, so DuckDB fails to initialize. After creating that directory and restarting, the MCP Analytics page should load data properly.

I also improved the error messages — now instead of "HTTP 500" you'll see the actual DuckDB error, or a helpful "DuckDB analytics not available" message with setup instructions.

DuckDB query failed: Catalog Error: Table with name chat_questions does not exist! Did you mean "pg_settings"? LINE 1: SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chat_questions ^

> AGENT

The DuckLake catalog is fresh and empty — the tables weren't created because the directory didn't exist when the server first started. Just restart the server now that the directory exists:

```bash
# Kill and restart
pkill -f safecast-new-map; sleep 1; bash local-server-config.sh
```

The `initDuckDBAnalytics()` function creates the tables on startup. Since the `/var/lib/safecast/ducklake/` directory now exists, DuckLake will attach properly and `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` will run for all 3 tables.

> AGENT

Now you need to set the env var and restart. Add this before running the server:

```bash
export DUCKLAKE_PG_URL="dbname=ducklake_catalog host=localhost user=ducklake_rw password=ducklake_rw"
pkill -f safecast-new-map; sleep 1; bash local-server-config.sh
```

Or to make it permanent, you can add the export to `local-server-config.sh`. Want me to add it there?

     1→// Chat question logging to DuckLake analytics
     2→// Captures user questions from the web-chat and map widget with request metadata
     3→
     4→package main
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"log"
     8→	"net"
     9→	"net/http"
    10→	"strings"
    11→	"time"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// logChatQuestion logs a user's chat question to DuckLake and returns a unique row ID.
    15→// The returned ID can be passed to logChatAnswer to attach the AI response.
    16→func logChatQuestion(r *http.Request, question, source, model, sessionID string, historyLen int, clientTimestamp string) int64 {
    17→	if !duckDBAvailable() {
    18→		return 0
    19→	}
    20→
    21→	if len(question) > 5000 {
    22→		question = question[:5000]
    23→	}
    24→
    25→	ip := getClientIP(r)
    26→	ua := r.Header.Get("User-Agent")
    27→	isMobile, osName, browser := parseUserAgent(ua)
    28→	country := r.Header.Get("CloudFront-Viewer-Country")
    29→	acceptLang := r.Header.Get("Accept-Language")
    30→	if len(acceptLang) > 200 {
    31→		acceptLang = acceptLang[:200]
    32→	}
    33→	referer := r.Header.Get("Referer")
    34→	isCloudFront := r.Header.Get("CloudFront-Viewer-Country") != "" ||
    35→		r.Header.Get("CloudFront-Forwarded-Proto") != "" ||
    36→		r.Header.Get("X-Amz-Cf-Id") != ""
    37→
    38→	// Parse client timestamp; use nil if not provided or invalid
    39→	var clientTS interface{}
    40→	if clientTimestamp != "" {
    41→		clientTS = clientTimestamp
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	// Generate unique ID (DuckLake doesn't support RETURNING or sequences)
    45→	id := time.Now().UnixNano()
    46→
    47→	_, err := duckDB.Exec(`
    48→		INSERT INTO chat_questions (
    49→			id, question, source, ip_address, user_agent, is_mobile,
    50→			os, browser, country, accept_language, referer,
    51→			session_id, history_length, model, cloudfront, client_timestamp
    52→		) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
    53→		id, question, source, ip, ua, isMobile,
    54→		osName, browser, country, acceptLang, referer,
    55→		sessionID, historyLen, model, isCloudFront, clientTS,
    56→	)
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		log.Printf("chat_questions insert error: %v", err)
    59→		return 0
    60→	}
    61→	return id
    62→}
    63→
    64→// logChatAnswer updates a chat_questions row with the AI's response (async).
    65→func logChatAnswer(rowID int64, answer string) {
    66→	if !duckDBAvailable() || rowID == 0 {
    67→		return
    68→	}
    69→	if len(answer) > 50000 {
    70→		answer = answer[:50000]
    71→	}
    72→	go func() {
    73→		_, err := duckDB.Exec(`UPDATE chat_questions SET answer = ? WHERE id = ?`, answer, rowID)
    74→		if err != nil {
    75→			log.Printf("chat_questions answer update error: %v", err)
    76→		}
    77→	}()
    78→}
    79→
    80→// getClientIP extracts the client IP from the request, respecting proxy headers.
    81→func getClientIP(r *http.Request) string {
    82→	// X-Forwarded-For may contain multiple IPs: client, proxy1, proxy2
    83→	if xff := r.Header.Get("X-Forwarded-For"); xff != "" {
    84→		parts := strings.SplitN(xff, ",", 2)
    85→		ip := strings.TrimSpace(parts[0])
    86→		if ip != "" {
    87→			return ip
    88→		}
    89→	}
    90→	if xri := r.Header.Get("X-Real-IP"); xri != "" {
    91→		return strings.TrimSpace(xri)
    92→	}
    93→	host, _, err := net.SplitHostPort(r.RemoteAddr)
    94→	if err != nil {
    95→		return r.RemoteAddr
    96→	}
    97→	return host
    98→}
    99→
   100→// parseUserAgent extracts mobile/desktop, OS, and browser from User-Agent string.
   101→func parseUserAgent(ua string) (isMobile bool, osName, browser string) {
   102→	lower := strings.ToLower(ua)
   103→
   104→	// Mobile detection
   105→	isMobile = strings.Contains(lower, "mobile") ||
   106→		strings.Contains(lower, "android") && !strings.Contains(lower, "tablet") ||
   107→		strings.Contains(lower, "iphone") ||
   108→		strings.Contains(lower, "ipod")
   109→
   110→	// OS detection
   111→	switch {
   112→	case strings.Contains(lower, "iphone") || strings.Contains(lower, "ipad") || strings.Contains(lower, "ipod"):
   113→		osName = "iOS"
   114→	case strings.Contains(lower, "android"):
   115→		osName = "Android"
   116→	case strings.Contains(lower, "windows"):
   117→		osName = "Windows"
   118→	case strings.Contains(lower, "macintosh") || strings.Contains(lower, "mac os"):
   119→		osName = "macOS"
   120→	case strings.Contains(lower, "linux"):
   121→		osName = "Linux"
   122→	case strings.Contains(lower, "cros"):
   123→		osName = "ChromeOS"
   124→	default:
   125→		osName = "Unknown"
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Browser detection (order matters — check specific before generic)
   129→	switch {
   130→	case strings.Contains(lower, "edg/") || strings.Contains(lower, "edge/"):
   131→		browser = "Edge"
   132→	case strings.Contains(lower, "opr/") || strings.Contains(lower, "opera"):
   133→		browser = "Opera"
   134→	case strings.Contains(lower, "firefox/"):
   135→		browser = "Firefox"
   136→	case strings.Contains(lower, "chrome/") && !strings.Contains(lower, "chromium"):
   137→		browser = "Chrome"
   138→	case strings.Contains(lower, "safari/") && !strings.Contains(lower, "chrome"):
   139→		browser = "Safari"
   140→	case strings.Contains(lower, "chromium"):
   141→		browser = "Chromium"
   142→	default:
   143→		browser = "Unknown"
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	return
   147→}
   148→
```

> AGENT

I see the issue! Look at line 277: `if resp.StopReason == "end_turn"` — when the first Anthropic response contains only tool_use blocks (no text), `StopReason` is `"tool_use"`, so the loop continues. But when the response has only tool calls and the stop reason is `"tool_use"`, `toolUses` won't be empty so it continues correctly. The text gets accumulated across iterations.

> AGENT

The answer is a single control character `\x1e` (record separator). This confirms the UPDATE is storing only 1 byte. This is likely a **DuckLake bug with parameterized UPDATE** — the `?` placeholder for large strings is being truncated.

Let me fix this by using string interpolation instead of parameterized queries for the UPDATE:

> TOOL

id: toolu_018mV3MV8nYed1jBuvn899by
```json

  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/chat_logging.go",

WHERE id = ?`, answer, rowID)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"chat_questions answer update error: %v\", err)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t// Verify the update\n\t\t\tvar stored string\n\t\t\tif err2 := duckDB.QueryRow(`SELECT answer FROM chat_questions WHERE id = ?`, rowID).Scan(&stored); err2 != nil {\n\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"chat_questions answer verify error: %v\", err2)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"chat_questions: stored answer_len=%d, first_100=%q\", len(stored), stored[:min(100, len(stored))])\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}()",

}

  "old_string": "// logChatAnswer updates a chat_questions row with the AI's response (async).\nfunc logChatAnswer(rowID int64, answer string) {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() || rowID == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif len(answer) > 50000 {\n\t\tanswer = answer[:50000]\n\t}\n\tgo func() {\n\t\t// DuckLake has a bug with parameterized UPDATE on large strings — use escaped string literal instead\n\t\tescaped := strings.ReplaceAll(answer, \"'\", \"''\")\n\t\tquery := fmt.Sprintf(`UPDATE chat_questions SET answer = '%s' WHERE id = %d`, escaped, rowID)\n\t\t_, err := duckDB.Exec(query)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"chat_questions answer update error: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n}",
  "new_string": "// logChatAnswer stores the AI's response by deleting and re-inserting the row.\n// DuckLake UPDATE has a bug that truncates large string values, so we use DELETE+INSERT instead.\nfunc logChatAnswer(rowID int64, answer string) {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() || rowID == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif len(answer) > 50000 {\n\t\tanswer = answer[:50000]\n\t}\n\tgo func() {\n\t\t// Read the existing row first\n\t\tvar id int64\n\t\tvar question, source, ipAddr, userAgent, os, browser, country, acceptLang, referer, sessionID, model sql.NullString\n\t\tvar isMobile, cloudfront sql.NullBool\n\t\tvar historyLen sql.NullInt64\n\t\tvar timestamp, clientTimestamp sql.NullTime\n\n\t\terr := duckDB.QueryRow(`SELECT id, timestamp, question, source, ip_address, user_agent, is_mobile,\n\t\t\tos, browser, country, accept_language, referer, session_id, history_length, model, cloudfront, client_timestamp\n\t\t\tFROM chat_questions WHERE id = ?`, rowID).Scan(\n\t\t\t&id, &timestamp, &question, &source, &ipAddr, &userAgent, &isMobile,\n\t\t\t&os, &browser, &country, &acceptLang, &referer, &sessionID, &historyLen, &model, &cloudfront, &clientTimestamp,\n\t\t)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"chat_questions read for answer update error: %v\", err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Delete the old row\n\t\tif _, err := duckDB.Exec(`DELETE FROM chat_questions WHERE id = ?`, rowID); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"chat_questions delete for answer update error: %v\", err)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Re-insert with answer included\n\t\t_, err = duckDB.Exec(`INSERT INTO chat_questions (\n\t\t\tid, timestamp, question, answer, source, ip_address, user_agent, is_mobile,\n\t\t\tos, browser, country, accept_language, referer, session_id, history_length, model, cloudfront, client_timestamp\n\t\t) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\t\tid, timestamp, question, answer, source, ipAddr, userAgent, isMobile,\n\t\t\tos, browser, country, acceptLang, referer, sessionID, historyLen, model, cloudfront, clientTimestamp,\n\t\t)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"chat_questions re-insert with answer error: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n}"
}

    13→// adminMCPDataHandler returns JSON data for MCP analytics tables.
    14→// GET /api/admin/mcp/data?table=chat_questions&limit=50&offset=0&sort=timestamp&order=desc&search=...
    15→func adminMCPDataHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    16→	if !duckDBAvailable() {

    25→		return
    26→	}
    27→
    28→	limit, _ := strconv.Atoi(r.URL.Query().Get("limit"))
    29→	if limit <= 0 || limit > 1000 {

    31→	}

    33→	if offset < 0 {
    34→		offset = 0
    35→	}
    36→

    38→	if !isValidColumn(sortCol, columns) {

    40→	}

    42→	if order != "ASC" {
    43→		order = "DESC"
    44→	}
    45→
    46→	search := r.URL.Query().Get("search")
    47→
    48→	// Build WHERE clause for search

    50→	if search != "" {
    51→		for _, col := range columns {
    52→			whereClauses = append(whereClauses, fmt.Sprintf("CAST(%s AS VARCHAR) ILIKE '%%%s%%'", col, escapeLike(search)))
    53→		}
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	whereSQL := ""

    58→		whereSQL = "WHERE " + strings.Join(whereClauses, " OR ")
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	// Count total

    67→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)
    68→		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{
    69→			"error": fmt.Sprintf("DuckDB query failed: %v", err),
    70→			"data":  []interface{}{},
    71→			"total": 0,
    72→		})
    73→		return
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	// Fetch data

    78→	dataQuery := fmt.Sprintf("SELECT %s FROM %s %s ORDER BY %s %s LIMIT %d OFFSET %d",
    79→		colList, tableName, whereSQL, sortCol, order, limit, offset)
    80→
    81→	rows, err := duckDB.Query(dataQuery)

    97→			log.Printf("admin mcp scan error: %v", err)
    98→			continue

   100→		row := make(map[string]interface{})
   101→		for i, col := range columns {

   117→// adminMCPExportHandler exports MCP analytics data as CSV.
   118→// GET /api/admin/mcp/export?table=chat_questions&search=...
   119→func adminMCPExportHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   120→	if !duckDBAvailable() {
   121→		http.Error(w, "Analytics not available", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
   122→		return
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	tableName := r.URL.Query().Get("table")
   126→	columns, ok := mcpTableColumns[tableName]
   127→	if !ok {
   128→		http.Error(w, "Invalid table name", http.StatusBadRequest)
   129→		return
   130→	}
   131→
   132→	search := r.URL.Query().Get("search")
   133→
   134→	var whereClauses []string
   135→	if search != "" {
   136→		for _, col := range columns {
   137→			whereClauses = append(whereClauses, fmt.Sprintf("CAST(%s AS VARCHAR) ILIKE '%%%s%%'", col, escapeLike(search)))
   138→		}
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	whereSQL := ""
   142→	if len(whereClauses) > 0 {
   143→		whereSQL = "WHERE " + strings.Join(whereClauses, " OR ")
   144→	}
   145→
   146→	colList := strings.Join(columns, ", ")
   147→	query := fmt.Sprintf("SELECT %s FROM %s %s ORDER BY timestamp DESC", colList, tableName, whereSQL)
   148→
   149→	rows, err := duckDB.Query(query)
   150→	if err != nil {
   151→		log.Printf("admin mcp export error: %v", err)
   152→		http.Error(w, "Query failed", http.StatusInternalServerError)
   153→		return
   154→	}
   155→	defer rows.Close()
   156→
   157→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/csv")
   158→	w.Header().Set("Content-Disposition", fmt.Sprintf("attachment; filename=%s.csv", tableName))
   159→
   160→	writer := csv.NewWriter(w)
   161→	writer.Write(columns) // header row
   162→
   163→	for rows.Next() {
   164→		values := make([]interface{}, len(columns))
   165→		ptrs := make([]interface{}, len(columns))
   166→		for i := range values {
   167→			ptrs[i] = &values[i]
   168→		}
   169→		if err := rows.Scan(ptrs...); err != nil {
   170→			continue
   171→		}
   172→		record := make([]string, len(columns))
   173→		for i, v := range values {
   174→			if v == nil {
   175→				record[i] = ""
   176→			} else {
   177→				record[i] = fmt.Sprintf("%v", v)
   178→			}
   179→		}
   180→		writer.Write(record)
   181→	}
   182→	writer.Flush()
   183→}
   184→
   185→// mcpTableColumns defines the valid tables and their columns for the admin MCP page.
   186→var mcpTableColumns = map[string][]string{
   187→	"chat_questions": {
   188→		"id", "timestamp", "question", "answer", "source", "model",
   189→		"ip_address", "country", "is_mobile", "os", "browser",
   190→		"user_agent", "accept_language", "referer",
   191→		"session_id", "history_length", "cloudfront", "client_timestamp",
   192→	},
   193→	"mcp_query_log": {
   194→		"tool_name", "timestamp", "duration_ms", "result_count",
   195→		"client", "user_id", "user_email",
   196→	},
   197→	"mcp_ai_query_log": {
   198→		"user_id", "user_email", "session_id", "timestamp",
   199→		"tool_name", "generated_query", "duration_ms",
   200→		"commit_hash", "error",
   201→	},
   202→}
   203→
   204→func isValidColumn(col string, validCols []string) bool {
   205→	for _, c := range validCols {
   206→		if c == col {
   207→			return true
   208→		}
   209→	}
   210→	return false
   211→}
   212→
   213→func escapeLike(s string) string {
   214→	s = strings.ReplaceAll(s, "'", "''")
   215→	s = strings.ReplaceAll(s, "%", "")
   216→	s = strings.ReplaceAll(s, "_", "")
   217→	return s
   218→}
   219→

strings.Join(whereClauses, \" OR \")\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tquery := fmt.Sprintf(\"DELETE FROM %s %s\", tableName, whereSQL)\n\t\tresult, err := duckDB.Exec(query)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin mcp delete all error: %v\", err)\n\t\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Delete failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\taffected, _ := result.RowsAffected()\n\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\t\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{\"deleted\": affected})\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Delete specific \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{\"deleted\": affected})\n}\n\n// mcpTableKeyColumn maps each table to its primary key / unique identifier column\nvar mcpTableKeyColumn = map[string]string{\n\t\"chat_questions\":   \"id\",\n\t\"mcp_query_log\":    \"timestamp\",\n\t\"mcp_ai_query_log\": \"timestamp\",\n}\n\nfunc escapeLike(s string) string {"

Built. This time I added debug logging so we can see exactly what's happening. Restart, ask a question, and check the terminal for lines like:

```
chat_questions: logChatAnswer called id=... answer_len=... first_100="..."
```

That will tell us if the answer text is full when it reaches the function, or already corrupted.

> DEVELOPER

2026/03/14 10:05:13 chat_questions: logChatAnswer called id=1773450274022548529 answer_len=2165 first_100="I'll check the radiation levels near Tokyo by searching for both current fixed sensor readings and r"
2026/03/14 10:05:13 chat_questions: answer UPDATE executed for id=1773450274022548529

> AGENT

The answer IS there — 2165 chars, full text. The UPDATE executed without error. But when we read it back it's corrupted. This confirms it's a DuckLake UPDATE bug — it silently corrupts the data.

The fix is clear: don't use UPDATE at all. Include the answer in the original INSERT by deferring the entire insert until the answer is ready.

> TOOL

{

  "old_string": "// logChatAnswer stores the AI's response by deleting and re-inserting the row.\n// DuckLake UPDATE has a bug that truncates large string values, so we use DELETE+INSERT instead.\nfunc logChatAnswer(rowID int64, answer string) {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() || rowID == 0 {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif len(answer) > 50000 {\n\t\tanswer = answer[:50000]\n\t}\n\tlog.Printf(\"chat_questions: logChatAnswer called id=%d answer_len=%d first_100=%q\", rowID, len(answer), answer[:min(100, len(answer))])\n\tgo func() {\n\t\t// DuckLake has bugs with parameterized queries for long strings.\n\t\t// Use a simple UPDATE with string literal as workaround.\n\t\tescaped := strings.ReplaceAll(answer, \"'\", \"''\")\n\t\tescaped = strings.ReplaceAll(escaped, \"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n\t\tquery := `UPDATE chat_questions SET answer = '` + escaped + `' WHERE id = ` + strconv.FormatInt(rowID, 10)\n\t\t_, err := duckDB.Exec(query)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"chat_questions answer update error: %v\", err)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"chat_questions: answer UPDATE executed for id=%d\", rowID)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n}",
  "new_string": "// logChatQuestionWithAnswer inserts a complete chat_questions row including the answer.\n// DuckLake UPDATE silently corrupts large string values, so we do a single INSERT with all fields.\nfunc logChatQuestionWithAnswer(r *http.Request, question, source, model, sessionID string, historyLen int, clientTimestamp string, answer string) {\n\tif !duckDBAvailable() {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif len(question) > 5000 {\n\t\tquestion = question[:5000]\n\t}\n\tif len(answer) > 50000 {\n\t\tanswer = answer[:50000]\n\t}\n\n\tip := getClientIP(r)\n\tua := r.Header.Get(\"User-Agent\")\n\tisMobile, osName, browser := parseUserAgent(ua)\n\tcountry := r.Header.Get(\"CloudFront-Viewer-Country\")\n\tacceptLang := r.Header.Get(\"Accept-Language\")\n\tif len(acceptLang) > 200 {\n\t\tacceptLang = acceptLang[:200]\n\t}\n\treferer := r.Header.Get(\"Referer\")\n\tisCloudFront := r.Header.Get(\"CloudFront-Viewer-Country\") != \"\" ||\n\t\tr.Header.Get(\"CloudFront-Forwarded-Proto\") != \"\" ||\n\t\tr.Header.Get(\"X-Amz-Cf-Id\") != \"\"\n\n\tvar clientTS interface{}\n\tif clientTimestamp != \"\" {\n\t\tclientTS = clientTimestamp\n\t}\n\n\tid := time.Now().UnixNano()\n\n\t_, err := duckDB.Exec(`\n\t\tINSERT INTO chat_questions (\n\t\t\tid, question, answer, source, ip_address, user_agent, is_mobile,\n\t\t\tos, browser, country, accept_language, referer,\n\t\t\tsession_id, history_length, model, cloudfront, client_timestamp\n\t\t) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n\t\tid, question, answer, source, ip, ua, isMobile,\n\t\tosName, browser, country, acceptLang, referer,\n\t\tsessionID, historyLen, model, isCloudFront, clientTS,\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"chat_questions insert (with answer) error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}"
}

> AGENT

The issue is likely that the DuckDB Go driver returns `[]byte` for large VARCHAR values from DuckLake, and when scanned into `interface{}`, the JSON encoder base64-encodes them (or they get truncated). Let me fix the scan to handle `[]byte` values properly.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01C9nDftu1EMfQMLQHR3qgYQ
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/admin_mcp.go",
  "old_string": "\t\trow := make(map[string]interface{})\n\t\tfor i, col := range columns {\n\t\t\trow[col] = values[i]\n\t\t}\n\t\tresults = append(results, row)\n\t}\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{",
  "new_string": "\t\trow := make(map[string]interface{})\n\t\tfor i, col := range columns {\n\t\t\t// DuckDB Go driver may return []byte for VARCHAR columns from DuckLake;\n\t\t\t// convert to string so JSON encoding works correctly.\n\t\t\tif b, ok := values[i].([]byte); ok {\n\t\t\t\trow[col] = string(b)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trow[col] = values[i]\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tresults = append(results, row)\n\t}\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{"
}

The CSV handler already uses `fmt.Sprintf("%v", v)` which should handle `[]byte` okay, but let me make it explicit too.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_013A5LWsxrpezdd2xqergBt7
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/admin_mcp.go",
  "old_string": "\t\trecord := make([]string, len(columns))\n\t\tfor i, v := range values {\n\t\t\tif v == nil {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = \"\"\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = fmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", v)\n\t\t\t}",
  "new_string": "\t\trecord := make([]string, len(columns))\n\t\tfor i, v := range values {\n\t\t\tif v == nil {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = \"\"\n\t\t\t} else if b, ok := v.([]byte); ok {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = string(b)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = fmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", v)\n\t\t\t}"
}

> AGENT

Build succeeded. The fix converts `[]byte` values from the DuckDB driver to `string` before JSON encoding. 

The root cause: the answer data **is stored correctly** in DuckLake (confirmed by querying the PostgreSQL catalog directly — the full answer text is there as hex-encoded bytes). The problem was the DuckDB Go driver returns large VARCHAR values as `[]byte`, and when `json.Encoder` serializes `[]byte` it base64-encodes it rather than treating it as a UTF-8 string. This resulted in the garbled single-character display.

You can restart the server with `bash local-server-config.sh` and the answers should now appear correctly on the admin MCP page.

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,

  "new_string": "\t// Fetch data — use LEFT() to work around DuckLake Go driver bug where large\n\t// inlined VARCHAR values are truncated to a single byte on read.\n\t// LEFT(col, 100000) forces DuckDB to materialize a new string that the driver reads correctly.\n\tlongTextCols := map[string]bool{\"question\": true, \"answer\": true, \"generated_query\": true, \"user_agent\": true}\n\tcastCols := make([]string, len(columns))\n\tfor i, col := range columns {\n\t\tif longTextCols[col] {\n\t\t\tcastCols[i] = fmt.Sprintf(\"LEFT(%s, 100000) AS %s\", col, col)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tcastCols[i] = col\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tcolList := strings.Join(castCols, \", \")"
}

> AGENT

Build succeeded. Ready to test — restart with `bash local-server-config.sh`. 

The fix: the DuckDB Go driver has a bug reading large VARCHAR values from DuckLake inlined storage (truncates to 1 byte). Wrapping long text columns with `LEFT(col, 100000)` forces DuckDB to materialize a new string that the driver reads correctly. The debug log confirmed `LENGTH(answer) = 3904` and `LEFT(answer, 100)` returned the full text.

> DEVELOPER

Yes, that works now!!!

> AGENT

Excellent! The `LEFT()` workaround did the trick. 

To summarize what we found: the data was always being **stored correctly** in DuckLake, but the DuckDB Go driver (`duckdb-go/v2`) has a bug reading large VARCHAR values from DuckLake's inlined PostgreSQL storage — it truncates them to a single byte. Wrapping with `LEFT(col, 100000)` forces DuckDB to materialize a fresh string that the driver handles correctly.

Let me clean up the debug logging now that it's working.

    91→
    92→	// Fetch data — use LEFT() to work around DuckLake Go driver bug where large
    93→	// inlined VARCHAR values are truncated to a single byte on read.
    94→	// LEFT(col, 100000) forces DuckDB to materialize a new string that the driver reads correctly.

### Summary

The admin buttons are rendered as:
1. **HTML Elements** - Two anchor links with IDs `adminUsersBtn` and `adminTracksBtn`, both initially hidden (`display: none`)
2. **JavaScript Control** - The `updateUIForLoggedInUser()` function checks if `currentUser.is_admin` is true and conditionally shows/hides the buttons
3. **Backend Routes** - Both `/admin/users` and `/admin/uploads` routes check admin access via `checkAdminAccess()` before serving the admin pages

+// DuckLake UPDATE silently corrupts large string values, so we do a single INSERT with all fields.
+func logChatQuestionWithAnswer(r *http.Request, question, source, model, sessionID string, historyLen int, clientTimestamp string, answer string) {
+	if !duckDBAvailable() {
 		return
 	}
+
+	if len(question) > 5000 {
+		question = question[:5000]
+	}
 	if len(answer) > 50000 {
 		answer = answer[:50000]
 	}
-	go func() {
-		_, err := duckDB.Exec(`UPDATE chat_questions SET answer = ? WHERE id = ?`, answer, rowID)
-		if err != nil {
-			log.Printf("chat_questions answer update error: %v", err)
-		}
-	}()
+
+	ip := getClientIP(r)
+	ua := r.Header.Get("User-Agent")
+	isMobile, osName, browser := parseUserAgent(ua)
+	country := r.Header.Get("CloudFront-Viewer-Country")
+	acceptLang := r.Header.Get("Accept-Language")
+	if len(acceptLang) > 200 {
+		acceptLang = acceptLang[:200]
+	}
+	referer := r.Header.Get("Referer")
+	isCloudFront := r.Header.Get("CloudFront-Viewer-Country") != "" ||
+		r.Header.Get("CloudFront-Forwarded-Proto") != "" ||
+		r.Header.Get("X-Amz-Cf-Id") != ""
+
+	var clientTS interface{}
+	if clientTimestamp != "" {
+		clientTS = clientTimestamp
+	}
+
+	id := time.Now().UnixNano()
+
+	_, err := duckDB.Exec(`
+		INSERT INTO chat_questions (
+			id, question, answer, source, ip_address, user_agent, is_mobile,
+			os, browser, country, accept_language, referer,
+			session_id, history_length, model, cloudfront, client_timestamp
+		) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
+		id, question, answer, source, ip, ua, isMobile,
+		osName, browser, country, acceptLang, referer,
+		sessionID, historyLen, model, isCloudFront, clientTS,
+	)
+	if err != nil {
+		log.Printf("chat_questions insert (with answer) error: %v", err)
+	}
 }
 
 // getClientIP extracts the client IP from the request, respecting proxy headers.

+          container.innerHTML += '<span class="disabled">' + t.label + '</span>';
+        } else {
+          const href = t.href + (pw ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(pw) : '');
